## Implementation Notes: Dependencies and Parallelization

### Mandatory Serial Main Chain

`P0 → P1 → P2 → P3 → P3.5 → P4 → P5 → P6 → (P7 ∥ P8) → P9 → P10`

> **P3 hemorrhage-control path (ruling of 2026-10-05)**: P3’s prerequisites are narrowed to
> **2.3.2 + 2.4.1 + 2.4.3** (multi-file corpus layer + two red criteria). The rest of P2 (2.1 IR
> verifier, 2.2 snapshots, 2.3.1/2.3.3 differential and performance baseline) may proceed in
> parallel with P3. Stopping correctness vulnerabilities from bleeding is not blocked by criterion
> infrastructure; however, **P4 must still wait for all of P2**—the unified Driver requires all
> three layers of criteria to be in place.

| Constraint                | Reason                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| P0 → P1                   | The three prohibitions constrain every action in P1 (1.2.x adds `mod` declarations to `mod.rs`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| P1 → P2                   | Reviving tests changes the test count and corpus baseline                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| P2 (after narrowing) → P3 | Vulnerability criteria must be **written red first** (2.4.1/2.4.3), and the multi-file corpus layer (2.3.2) must exist so that the red tests have somewhere to run                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| P3 → P4                   | Fix bugs before refactoring; reversing the order would cause bugs to be frozen into the phase table as “established behavior”                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| P3.5 → P4                 | RFC-029g’s (#399) deletion surface covers P4 4.2.2 / P5 / P6 / P7 / P8 rewrite targets (the list is in §P3.5 of this document)—reversing the order means repeating the same deletions on the new grammar, new checker layout, and new IR (D56)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| P4 → P5                   | The `include!` refactor and checker split change the same file                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| P5 → P6                   | Consecutive changes to the same file must be separate, or regressions cannot be bisected                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| P6 → P7 / P8              | If type representations do not converge first, the new IR will grow into a third representation; **the P7 gate = the stage A/B/C exits of 6.9** (I1–I3, I5, I7; namely 6.9.2 including C3, 6.9.3 including d, and completion of 6.9.4), while D (Σ) does not block; P7 may consume only quantifier-derived views and the defeq interface and must not establish a parallel ledger; the 7.0 pure-move batch is a prerequisite for P7, is not subject to this gate, and must not consume the undecided defeq/quantifier interfaces |
| P7/P8 → P9                | The final form of 9.5’s snapshot baseline and 9.3’s `pub(crate)` leak count is taken only after P7/P8                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

### Parallelization Groups

| Group                                        | Members                                                                            | Basis                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| -------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **A (the only group authorized by the RFC)** | P7 ∥ P8                                                                            | Disjoint file sets: `ir.rs`/`ir_gen.rs`/`bytecode.rs`/`translator.rs` vs `lexer/*`/`parser/*`                                                                                                                                                       |
| B                                            | 1.2.1 ∥ 1.2.2 ∥ 1.2.3 ∥ 1.2.4                                                      | Each independently adds a `mod`; revert file by file                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| C                                            | 2.1 ∥ 2.2 ∥ 2.3.1 ∥ 2.3.2 ∥ 2.4                                                    | The three layers of criteria are independent                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| D                                            | 4.4 (proof-layer cleanup) ∥ the entire P6 line                                     | `02` explicitly states under S5 that it “should proceed independently of S1-S4”                                                                                                                                                                     |
| E                                            | 4.2.5 ∥ 4.2.6 ∥ 4.2.8                                                              | All become mutually independent after 4.1                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| F                                            | 9.3 ∥ 9.9 ∥ 9.10 (the three scripts introduced by P0 may be developed in parallel) | 9.5’s snapshot baseline must be taken last                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| G                                            | S3 ∥ S4 ∥ S5                                                                       | Disjoint file sets (verified 2026-10-05: S3 changes `docs/src/dev/design/check/` + `config.js`; S4 changes `docs/src/rfc/` + the `check_tracking.py` generator; S5 changes two `pub` locations in `src/frontend/core/typecheck/`; no pair overlaps) |

**Cannot run in parallel (file overlap)**: P1(1.1.x) ∥ P8(8.0.x); 1.2.1 ∥ 8.3.2; 6.0 ∥ S2 (B6/E5 in
the same batch); 4.3.2 ∥ 9.2 (unified under P4); **3.4 ∥ P3.5** (`checker.rs` / `environment.rs` are
the same files—the external track’s pub-deletion batch and the silent-channel fixes must be serial).

**New internal document constraints**: 6.0→6.1→6.2→6.3→6.4→6.5→6.6 is strictly serial; 7.0 →
7a→7b→7c→7d is strictly serial (7.0 is P7’s pure-move prerequisite batch), and 7b’s `Phi` arm must
precede 7d.

## Key Decisions and Rationale

- **P0 is an independent phase rather than part of P9**—gates are “after-the-fact checks,” while
  rules are “before-the-fact judgments.” P9’s scripts cannot prevent “patching when a refactor was
  required”; only D0–D4’s review checklist can do that.
- **P9 is the sole authoritative list of CI scripts**—the three overlapping lists created by gap G3
  made closure impossible. This table merges them, assigning each of the 10 active scripts to one
  phase (9.8 was cancelled by the user on 2026-11-24), with report-only status and hard-failure
  timing explicitly traceable.
- **P3.5 incorporates RFC-029g rather than leaving it as an “independent track to execute after the
  refactor”**—its deletion surface overlaps the rewrite surfaces of P4/P5/P6/P7/P8 (RFC-039 D56).
  Deferring it until after the refactor means repeating the same deletions on the new grammar, new
  checker layout, and new IR; this is the same lesson as incorporating `2315`/`2341` into 7e and RFC
  status corrections into S4.
- **P5’s steps are supplied by this document rather than merged into P4**—P4 is already the largest
  single-point risk, and adding the checker split would exceed a practically revertible granularity.
- **5.3.2 uses “zero caller changes” as its hard acceptance criterion**—the split must be
  independently revertible.

## Known Limitations and Risks

- **1.3.x (fixing exposed defects) has no regression criterion**—it only says that “fixed defects
  must not be rolled back” and lacks a requirement to record what was fixed. **Execution
  convention**: the commit message for every 1.3.x fix must reference the fixed test name and defect
  description, providing evidence for later bisecting.
- ~~**7b.1’s granularity is too coarse**~~ **Fixed (2026-10-05)**: split into the exclusive commits
  7b.1a / 7b.1b per DoD 6.
- **8.2.1’s granularity is too coarse**—approximately 89 references are renamed, and missing one
  will **silently change associativity**.
- ~~**D17 / D20 have no corresponding level-3 tasks**~~ **Fixed (2026-10-05)**: D17 landed as 7e.1,
  and D20 landed as 4.5.1/4.5.2; D32/D34’s opcode reclamation was incorporated into 7e.1, with the
  deletion action left in P10 S6.
- ~~**The `2315` / `2341` data-loss defects were originally assigned to “separate issues”**~~
  **Fixed (2026-10-05)**: incorporated as 7e.2 / 7e.3. The exception table (throw/try) is core
  language semantics and must not be left for future implementation.
- ~~**E2 (`lib.rs:568/582` `todo!()`) has no phase ownership**~~ **Fixed (2026-10-05)**: merged into
  P10 S2.
- ~~**F7 (opcode gate extractor) has ambiguous ownership**~~ **Fixed (2026-10-05)**: merged into P10
  S6’s generate-time gate task.

## Conflict Registry: All Adjudicated

> **The original “Conflict Registry” C1–C8 and the open questions in each document have all
> converged into the [RFC-039 decision registry](../../rfc/accepted/039-compiler-architecture.md)
> (D1–D50).** This document contains no pending items. The item-by-item mapping is:

| Original ID | Issue                                                                               | Ruling                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| C1          | `precedence_inline.rs` is revived in P1 and deleted in P8                           | **Revive** (P1’s premise is that the revived tests contain meaningful assertions); P8 no longer deletes the file (Pratt is retained)                                            |
| C2          | “Grammar-driven” commitment vs. table-driven implementation                         | **Fallback approach**: retain Pratt + one declarative precedence table + same-source cross-checking of two `BinOp` implementations (the LALRPOP route is withdrawn); see P8 8.3 |
| C3          | Conflict between the C6 category definition and S1’s classification                 | **S1 belongs to C6** (revival requires only regression tests, not equivalence criteria); C6 is expanded to “pure deletion or pure revival”                                      |
| C4          | `test_release_plan_spans_consumed` whitelist                                        | **No whitelist**; the difference set must be empty. If it cannot be empty, the `ReleasePlan` contract is defective → change it to `PlanId` (D20/D41)                            |
| C5          | Internal differences between the two `Aggregation` functions have not been verified | **P4’s 4.2.7 has made it a mandatory verification item**; confirm line by line during implementation                                                                            |
| C6          | The wasm playground fix requires P4’s `ProgramKind`                                 | **The wasm-path vulnerability fix is merged into 4.2.8**; P3 handles only the three native entry points                                                                         |
| C7          | Ownership of `method_def_ordinals`                                                  | **Assign to 04** (P7 batch c), see D21                                                                                                                                          |
| C8          | Correction of `upvalue_count` and the `.42` version number                          | **Fix + change `VERSION` from 4 to 5** (the format header already has `MAGIC` + `VERSION` fields, `codegen/bytecode.rs:14-16`), see D17                                         |
